Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in forecasting theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 262 and the invariant I(A,B) = 367 mod 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to forecasting theory, we decompose the problem into 262 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 367. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by forecasting generators. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 167. QED.
